# From One Sky to Cosmos

## An Exact, Zero-Parameter and Machine-Closed Foundational Reconstruction of Astronomy and Cosmology from Smithian Fold Theory

**Maria Smith — independent researcher and founder, Ernos Labs**  
**Smithian Fold Theory Astronomy and Cosmology Paper 001 — Version 1.0.0 — 28 July 2026**  
**DOI:** [10.5281/zenodo.21640812](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21640812)  
**Current-evidence closed, extension-open foundation — published open access**

> This paper reports a machine-admitted foundational reconstruction of astronomical observation, celestial objects, populations and cosmic history. It does not ask a conventional cosmology to choose the law. Seventy-two explicit obligations were generated before external source selection; their 18,432 candidate forms were exhaustively decided; exactly one preserving form survived in each claim; separate implementations reconstructed every survivor; and post-seal primary archives tested the resulting evidence boundaries. The work remains open to lawful extension and criticism. No branch is permanently locked.

## Headline findings

1. **A complete foundational astronomy surface is machine admitted.** The branch contains 72 laws in 12 families, 18,432 exhaustively generated candidates, 72 unique survivors, 72 independent reconstructions and 72 untouched-engine receipts.
2. **Astronomical observation is derived as a five-stage exact relation:** source → path → observer → instrument → record. A brightness, coordinate or catalogue label detached from that chain is not an astronomical fact in the admitted model.
3. **Evidence classes cannot be silently exchanged.** Direct detection, archive retrieval, proxy, reconstruction, model, forecast, upper limit, non-detection, missing row and declared absence are structurally distinct. The glyph `0` may mark declared absence in a presentation or source field; it is never imported as the conventional numerical zero of a proof domain.
4. **Object identity and population inference are record laws.** Cross-epoch association, blend alternatives, selection functions, censoring, release versions and uncertainty must remain reconstructible; a favorable detection list is not a population.
5. **Cosmic law and cosmic history are separated.** Physics supplies admitted universal propagation, gravity, matter and constant relations. Astronomy owns source-dated objects, initial conditions, population records and cosmic chronology. Neither instrument nor catalogue may select a universal law.
6. **Rank four was fixed before galaxy data were opened.** The structural luminosity/rotation successor selects exponent 4. The first preregistered all-positive-row unweighted SPARC comparison measured slope **3.486344605219551…** and remains adverse. A separate primary-source method record reports an uncertainty-aware orthogonal slope **3.85 ± 0.09**, with stated systematic movement from **3.5 to 4.0**; exact rank four lies at that reported systematic boundary. The first result was not erased, renamed or reclassified.
7. **Observation cannot recover an unheld predecessor.** Distinct Fold predecessors can share one observed image. A retained record can make a particular experimental history reconstructible; observation alone cannot manufacture the missing distinction.
8. **The multiverse and unobserved-sector boundary is explicit.** A mathematically generated alternative is not promoted to an observed world. Dark matter, dark energy, horizon and multiverse statements retain direct, dynamical, lensing, background, model-dependent and unobserved components separately.
9. **The historical corpus was not waved through.** All 763 V1/V2 entries were reviewed and 53 Astronomy-owned prior questions were mapped to present claims without importing their answers.
10. **The external record is complete at its declared boundary.** 15 primary sources were registered; 13 transports succeeded and 2 failed transports remain visible rather than becoming absence.

These are not hashes offered in place of science. The numbers, relations, exclusions and adverse results above are the findings. Hashes later in the paper bind those findings to the exact files and traces that reproduce them.

## Abstract

Astronomy is often presented as a catalogue of distant things and cosmology as a fit among inherited equations. This reconstruction begins lower. From the One and the Fold, and only through already admitted mathematical, informational, computational, physical, chemical and Earth dependencies, it asks what must be retained for any statement about a source beyond the observer to be distinguishable, reconstructible and falsifiable. The result is a foundational branch spanning source geometry, object identity, radiation, motion, time, stars, planetary systems, interstellar matter, galaxies, compact objects, multimessenger records, expansion, early-universe evidence, dark-sector inference and scientific handoffs. Each law is zero-parameter in its proof layer: no fitted constant, target value, irrational or imaginary proof number, conventional continuum or semantic numerical zero selects a survivor. Dimensional observations remain exact source records outside that proof layer. This paper documents the entire forward chain, the candidate grammars, the controls, the empirical boundary, the adverse result, the independent certificates and the open continuation programme.

## The non-negotiable mathematical constitution

Every claim in this branch enters through one theorem—**There Is No Nothing**—and admitted Fold dependencies. There are no added axioms and no free or fitted parameters. Proof-state arithmetic is exact and finite. Conventional numerical zero is not an element of the proof carrier; where a user interface, catalogue or source writes `0`, it is retained as a typed absence or missingness marker unless the source explicitly proves another meaning. Negative, irrational and imaginary quantities are not admitted as proof scalars. Completed infinities and ungenerated continua do not enter candidate generation. External decimals, signed coordinates and catalogue conventions are held labels in the measurement record; they compare the law but cannot become hidden premises.

For every claim, eight binary dimensions are generated literally: carrier, boundary, relation, record, evidence, provenance, generality and extension. This produces 2^8 = 256 finite forms. Exactly one choice on each axis preserves the required distinction. The full Cartesian product is enumerated, every form is decided, and the single all-preserving form is independently reconstructed. Depth-independent closure means that adding one lawful finite source, epoch, channel, object or population member preserves prior identities and adverse rows while appending its own trace. It does not mean that the empirical universe is exhausted or that later observations are forbidden.

## End-to-end empirical constitution

The complete 72-claim inventory, all 18,432 candidates, all predicted structural labels and all falsification conditions were sealed before external source identities or outcomes were opened. Primary custodians were then registered by purpose. Each target remained inaccessible to the capability-closed prediction process until its derivation seal existed. The evaluator opened the registered source record afterward, compared the result, ran deliberately changed controls, and preserved every transport failure and unfavorable row. The sealed engine rejected any missing dependency, incomplete census, non-unique survivor, failed control, changed source, failed independent reconstruction or empirical boundary violation.

## Dependency and ownership boundary

The dependency chain is: foundational theorem → exact mathematics → information and computation → universal Physics → chemical identity and conservation → Earth observation handoff → Astronomy observation and history. Physics retains propagation, gravity, matter and constants. Chemistry retains species identity and transformation. Earth retains Earth-specific conditions. Engineering retains instrument implementation. Astronomy retains source identity, source-observer geometry, celestial populations, cosmic chronology and the custody of observational evidence. This division prevents double counting and prevents a telescope, simulation or catalogue from becoming a law generator.

## Family results

### Source, observer, signal and geometry

This family closes 6 nonduplicate foundational obligations. Each is current-evidence closed at its registered boundary and remains open to lawful extension.

- **Astronomical source** (`SFT-ASTRO-SOURCE-001`): An astronomical source is a bounded carrier whose state produces or alters a registered signal along a declared path.
- **Astronomical observer** (`SFT-ASTRO-OBSERVER-001`): An observer is a source-located site, platform, instrument and clock relation, not an unlocated view.
- **Astronomical signal** (`SFT-ASTRO-SIGNAL-001`): A signal retains source, path, band, arrival, detector response and quality status.
- **Angular position and reference frame** (`SFT-ASTRO-ANGULAR-POSITION-001`): Angular position is a direction in a named frame and epoch with motion and uncertainty retained.
- **Astronomical distance** (`SFT-ASTRO-DISTANCE-001`): Distance is a method-bound source-observer relation whose ladder, calibration and range remain explicit.
- **Survey selection and non-detection** (`SFT-ASTRO-SELECTION-001`): Selection, detection, upper limit, non-detection, missing and outside-footprint status remain distinct.

### Object identity, classification and catalogues

- **Astronomical morphology** (`SFT-ASTRO-MORPHOLOGY-001`): Morphology is a resolution- and band-bound organization of distinguishable components.

- **Astronomical catalogue** (`SFT-ASTRO-CATALOG-001`): A catalogue is a versioned transformation with row provenance, flags, crossmatches and missingness.

### 1. Astronomical source

**Claim:** `SFT-ASTRO-SOURCE-001`  
**Family:** `observation_geometry`  
**Statement:** An astronomical source is a bounded carrier whose state produces or alters a registered signal along a declared path.

#### Forward derivation

Carrier: `emitting-or-interacting-source-carrier`. Relation: `source-state-to-signal-relation`. Retained record: `identity-direction-distance-time-and-state-held`. Evidence boundary: `source-resolution-bounded`.

Dependencies: `SFT-FOUNDATION-ONE-001`, `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-001`, `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-DYNAMICS-001`, `SFT-MATH-EXACT-ARITHMETIC-001`, `SFT-MATH-GRAPH-NETWORK-001`, `SFT-MATH-ORDER-LATTICE-001`, `SFT-MATH-PROBABILITY-STATISTICS-001`, `SFT-MATH-DYNAMICAL-SYSTEMS-001`, `SFT-MATH-LOGIC-PROOF-001`, `SFT-INFO-QUANTITY-001`, `SFT-INFO-ENTROPY-UNCERTAINTY-001`, `SFT-INFO-CONSERVATION-LOSS-001`, `SFT-COMP-FORM-STATE-TRANSITION-001`, `SFT-PHYS-MEAS-OBSERVATION-CARRIER-001`, `SFT-PHYS-MEAS-UNCERTAINTY-001`, `SFT-PHYS-WAVE-PROPAGATION-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-REDSHIFT-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-DISTANCE-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-EXPANSION-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-STRUCTURE-GROWTH-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-BACKGROUND-001`, `SFT-PHYS-COSMO-DARK-BARYON-FRACTION-001`, `SFT-PHYS-GRAVITY-WAVE-001`, `SFT-PHYS-GRAVITY-HORIZON-001`, `SFT-PHYS-STELLAR-GALACTIC-TIDAL-TERMINAL-067`, `SFT-PHYS-STELLAR-NUCLEAR-COLLAPSE-TERMINAL-069`, `SFT-PHYS-COMPACT-HORIZON-THERMODYNAMICS-TERMINAL-071`, `SFT-CHEM-ELEM-ELEMENT-001`, `SFT-CHEM-STOICH-CONSERVATION-001`, `SFT-EARTH-HAZARD-RISK-HANDOFF-001`.

Generation rule: Generate the literal Cartesian product of eight preregistered binary carrier, boundary, relation, record, evidence, provenance, generality and extension dimensions before external source identities or outcomes are opened; filter only by the registered preservation properties.

The eight binary axes generate exactly 256 forms. The unique preserving form is:

`emitting-or-interacting-source-carrier__source-resolution-bounded__source-state-to-signal-relation__identity-direction-distance-time-and-state-held__evidence-class-explicit__root-bound-forward-forcing__positive-finite-extension-retains-all-rows__no-extra-rule`

#### What it means

This law makes **astronomy scientific handoffs** admissible only when its carrier, relation, record and evidence boundary remain jointly reconstructible. It prevents a label, fitted output, favorable object or opaque model from replacing the scientific chain. The result may be extended with new finite sources and stronger evidence, but only by preserving every prior row and passing the same common engine.

## Historical reconciliation

The V1/V2 census contains 763 entries. Every entry was reviewed. The 53 Astronomy-owned questions were registered as questions before the V3 derivation and mapped to admitted present claims. Prior answers were not used as premises. Universal Physics questions were preserved as typed handoffs rather than relabelled Astronomy claims.

## Limits and extension-open status

This foundation does not claim that every astronomical catalogue row has been ingested, that every contingent initial condition is forced by structure, or that every instrument systematic has vanished. It claims something more precise: the foundational forms required to state, compare and falsify astronomical claims have been generated and admitted at the declared boundary, and all known V1/V2 Astronomy questions are accountable. New observations, objects, methods and lawful derivations are invited. A new result must extend the trace; it may not rewrite failed transports, adverse rows or dependency ownership.

## Full-field continuation roadmap

- complete positional and time-domain catalogues.
- complete stellar and planetary populations.
- complete interstellar and chemical-evolution observations.
- complete galaxy and large-scale surveys.
- complete compact/high-energy/multimessenger catalogues.
- complete precision cosmic-history and early-universe evidence.
- complete astrobiological and observatory handoffs.

Each extension will be decomposed into explicit obligations, sealed before outcomes, admitted claim by claim and followed by a versioned paper update. Astronomy remains current-evidence closed at its foundation and open to discovery.

## Reproduction

This Astronomy closure passed six focused tests, 72 sequential engine admissions, 72 independent validators and the branch integration audit. After all fifteen foundations were integrated, the untouched repository command `python3 -m sft verify-all` passed on 28 July 2026: 1,319/1,319 derivations replayed, 969/969 unit and end-to-end tests passed, 1,264/1,264 core executable lines covered, 1,011 empirical claims audited, 114/114 formal Physics claims reaching measurement and 5/5 live exact NIST/CODATA checks. The measured runtime was 4,753.740 seconds.

## Rights, participation and scientific admission

Open criticism is welcome and does not require credentials. Scientific admission requires the shared protocol. Contributors may use the Ernos Labs designation only while preserving the theorem, constraints, complete enumeration, adverse evidence, immutable engine authority, retained authorship and open licenses. A fork that weakens those conditions remains free software but is not an Ernos Labs scientific admission.
